- Configuration: reused core-only `build-v030-component-effects`, Windows x64, Visual Studio 18 2026, MSBuild `18.9.1+a81b43525`, MSVC `19.51.36256.0`, Windows SDK `10.0.26100.0`, CMake/CTest `4.4.3`; Debug and Release, tests/examples ON, fonts/Vulkan OFF. No optional dependency discovery/download, GPU or native window.
- Procedure: `cmake -S . -B build-v030-component-effects -G "Visual Studio 18 2026" -A x64 -DTESSERA_BUILD_VULKAN=OFF -DTESSERA_BUILD_FONTS=OFF`; `cmake --build build-v030-component-effects --config Debug --parallel 1` and the Release equivalent. Full checks: `ctest --test-dir build-v030-component-effects -C Debug --output-on-failure --output-log build-v030-component-effects/editor-debug-tests.log`; Release uses `-C Release --parallel 2` and `editor-release-tests.log`. Focused Debug checks select `-R '^editor_prototype$'`.
- Result: all 51 CTest checks passed in Debug and Release, including the editor fixture, tool panel and both independent source consumers. Final editor builds produced no compiler warnings. Documentation/link/ownership checks and `git diff --check` passed.
- Fixture: four ordinary component panels; runtime JSON/metadata round trip and preview subtree preservation; schema-selected authored string joined to accepted value and source bytes; shared pointer/keyboard/logical-gamepad/semantic command execution, disabled eligibility and stale semantic handle rejection; pinned row state/style, focus and host Inspector selection across reorder; loading/failure/retry/ready projection, superseded completion rejection and explicit latest-attempt once-only shutdown detachment.
- Artifacts: Debug/Release `tessera_editor_prototype.exe`, `editor-debug-tests.log`, `editor-release-tests.log`, CTest and per-consumer configure/build/test logs under ignored `build-v030-component-effects`; the executable emits assertion failures and a compact success summary. No images are produced.
- Limits: scripted in-process selection and observation-only Inspector, placeholder text, one fixed logical viewport; no editable text buffer, caret/selection editing, command edit history, source edit/reload transaction, async owner recreation, replay/capture comparison, CPU image, native editor, optional font/GPU, other-platform or performance validation. Those active acceptance steps remain in [current work](../roadmap/current.md).

## Editor string edit evidence — 2026-10-15

- Revision: uncommitted implementation on base `e264f3fd5e13157d993c02f734f86c6e6bf13b92`. Sources: [text_edit.hpp](../../include/tessera/input/text_edit.hpp), [text_edit.cpp](../../src/input/text_edit.cpp), [logical edit checks](../../tests/input/text_edit_tests.cpp), [editor prototype](../../examples/editor-prototype/main.cpp), [source consumer](../../tests/cmake/consumer/main.cpp) and [CMake registration](../../CMakeLists.txt). The [input contract](../architecture/input.md#implemented-logical-string-editing) owns the pure transform; the [editor consumer](../integrations/path-finder.md#implemented-in-process-panel-fixture) owns staging, publication and history.
- Configuration: reused core-only `build-v030-component-effects`, Windows x64, Visual Studio 18 2026, MSBuild `18.9.1+a81b43525`, MSVC `19.51.36256.0`, Windows SDK `10.0.26100.0`, CMake/CTest `4.4.3`; Debug and Release, tests/examples ON, fonts/Vulkan OFF. No optional dependencies, external font files, GPU or native window.
- Procedure: `cmake --build build-v030-component-effects --config Debug --parallel 1` and the Release equivalent; `ctest --test-dir build-v030-component-effects -C Debug --output-on-failure --output-log build-v030-component-effects/editor-edit-debug-tests.log` and the Release equivalent with `editor-edit-release-tests.log`. Final fixture changes rebuilt `tessera_editor_prototype` in each configuration and ran focused `-R '^(text_edit|editor_prototype)$'` checks with `editor-edit-focused-{debug,release}.log`.
- Result: all 52 CTest checks passed in Debug and Release, including both independent source consumers. Final focused checks passed in both configurations with no compiler warnings. Documentation/link/ownership checks and `git diff --check` passed.
- Demonstrated operation/minimum fix: the schema-selected Inspector string needed a bounded committed-text/caret/selection candidate boundary before host command execution. The pure scalar transform supplies only that operation; composition, clipboard and editable widget infrastructure were not promoted. Existing commands, gestures, component mounting, inspection, replay and CPU rendering suffice for the rest of the fixture without new subsystem APIs.
- Boundary checks: Japanese and supplementary UTF-8 scalar movement/deletion, reverse/extended selections and collapse, selected replacement accounting at 4096 bytes, empty-boundary no-ops, split/out-of-bounds indices, malformed/overlong UTF-8, CR/LF, unknown enums, malformed observations, stale owner/revision after selection-only changes, disabled/read-only policies and revision overflow rejection. Independent source imports link and execute scalar deletion and stale-revision rejection through `tessera::core`.
- Editor checks: normalized committed text and editing keys consume input; drafts render through ordinary components without source/history mutation; multiple edits join one node-free author-ID command gesture across full-tree generations. Commit records one host history entry; Escape and selection-owner changes cancel without history, retaining newer revisions. Compatible string source replacement stages complete ordinary output with copied focus and no mounting/publication, then accepts one generation and updates source/property observations. Invalid JSON and unsupported source topology reject with located diagnostics and unchanged source/generation. Pinned row state/lifetime, Inspector selection, focus and async observations survive the compatible label reload; stale semantic handles reject.
- Lifetime/capture checks: replacing the preview with an empty component removes its observation owner and host selection/editing state, delivers detachment once and rejects late completion. Recreating the same preview rejects the old attempt despite reused request/sequence, accepts a fresh completion and rejects the old editing owner. Shutdown queues and explicitly delivers its separate cleanup once. Fixed 760×320 logical viewport, scale one, explicit replay tick at 10 microseconds, placeholder shaping and a source-defined 5×7 scalar-bit synthetic rasterizer reproduce replay semantic/layout/action observations and CPU RGBA8 bytes exactly. Replay initial geometry equals accepted layout; the label change alters CPU pixels. Panel invariants run on ordinary staged/accepted inspection generations. No image files are written.
- Artifacts: Debug/Release binaries, the four logs above and per-consumer configure/build/test logs under ignored `build-v030-component-effects`; the executable prints a compact result and exits nonzero on failure.
- Limits: one-line scalar byte editing and one compatible selected-property source replacement; this run excludes source-authored removal/type replacement, covered in [source replacement evidence](#editor-source-replacement-evidence--2026-10-15). Host preview removal/recreation is distinct from source topology reload. No grapheme/BiDi caret, visual caret/selection geometry, composition/IME, clipboard, native editable control, serialized edit/readiness workflow, general migration/error fallback, automatic history/publication, real-font/GPU image, other-platform or performance claim. CPU capture replays accepted documents and action requests rather than executing editor command mutations. Source edits/images are in memory. Serialized formats, release metadata and dependencies are unchanged.

## Editor source replacement evidence — 2026-10-15

- Revision: uncommitted implementation on base `3b2672a53ecd565e677f6854a1b6b7695b2c4653`. Source and checks: [editor prototype](../../examples/editor-prototype/main.cpp), registered as `editor_prototype` in [CMake](../../CMakeLists.txt). The [editor consumer](../integrations/path-finder.md#implemented-in-process-panel-fixture) owns the bounded source compatibility and host acceptance boundary; core APIs and serialized formats are unchanged.
- Configuration: reused core-only `build-v030-component-effects`, Windows x64, Visual Studio 18 2026, MSBuild `18.9.1+a81b43525`, MSVC `19.51.36256.0`, Windows SDK `10.0.26100.0`, CMake/CTest `4.4.3`; Debug and Release, tests/examples ON, fonts/Vulkan OFF. No external dependencies, font assets, native window or GPU.
- Procedure: `cmake --build build-v030-component-effects --config Debug --parallel 1` and the Release equivalent; `ctest --test-dir build-v030-component-effects -C Debug --output-on-failure --output-log build-v030-component-effects/editor-source-debug-tests.log` and the Release equivalent with `editor-source-release-tests.log`. Final retained-owner cleanup/expired-state assertions rebuilt `tessera_editor_prototype` and ran focused Debug `-R '^editor_prototype$'` with `editor-source-focused-debug.log`.
- Result: all 52 CTest checks passed in Debug and Release, including both independent source consumers; the final focused Debug fixture also passed. Final builds emitted no compiler warnings. Documentation/link/ownership checks and `git diff --check` passed.
- Demonstrated operation/minimum fix: the compatible-string operation rejects source node removal/type change as `unsupported_reload`; it cannot exercise affected owner recovery. The fixture's separate structure operation admits only direct headline removal, Text-to-empty-Box replacement preserving other authored fields, and original Text restoration. It stages full ordinary lowering/eligibility/style/layout/copied focus/inspection/invariants/paint without mounting, projecting fresh defaults where kinds change; acceptance reconciles the exact staged records. Existing runtime boundaries suffice, so no generalized runtime reload or lifecycle extension was added.
- Rejection checks: malformed JSON, invalid node kind, unrelated source mutation, injected measurement failure and injected glyph shaping/paint failure return candidate-located diagnostics. The exact source bytes, preview document, command/inspection generations, focus, Inspector state, pinned weak state, async observation and owner lifetimes remain accepted; no owner cleanup is queued or delivered. Rejected paint staging preserves an open draft and gesture. Repeated ordinary capture after rejection reproduces the prior replay observations and CPU bytes.
- Acceptance checks: each removal/type change accepts exactly one generation, cancels an affected draft without history, clears incompatible Inspector selection/editing state, recovers Inspector/row focus to the retained subtitle row, and rejects stale semantic handles and direct selection of the incompatible author ID. Only the source-shape preview owner and removed/replaced headline row retire; old pinned state expires, unrelated row lifetime/state/style persist. Source restoration creates fresh headline/preview lifetimes and retains compatible subtitle selection/editing state while joining new authored source spans into both host and presented Inspector observations.
- Async/capture checks: the changed preview owner detaches its loading attempt once through explicit cleanup delivery; old handles and recreated observations reject old completion, including equal request/sequence. Fresh completion succeeds. Retained-owner cleanup runs once at shutdown. Fixed 760×320 logical viewport, scale one, explicit replay tick at 10 microseconds, placeholder shaping and the source-defined 5×7 scalar-bit rasterizer reproduce semantic/layout/action observations and CPU RGBA8 bytes across repeated captures; accepted removal/type replacement changes pixels and replay geometry equals accepted full-tree geometry. Source edits and images stay in memory.
- Artifacts: Debug/Release `tessera_editor_prototype.exe`, the three test logs above and per-consumer configure/build/test logs under ignored `build-v030-component-effects`. The executable prints a compact result and exits nonzero on a failed assertion; no image files are written.
- Limits: one direct label and three explicit source shapes with fixed host invocation/state schemas; no arbitrary topology/property migration, rollback of mounting/allocation/runtime failures or host callback side effects, source file writing, serialized editor workflow, native editor, platform editing, real-font/GPU image, other-platform or performance claim. Capture replays accepted documents and action requests rather than executing editor mutations. General mechanisms stay in [backlog](../roadmap/backlog.md#unscheduled-editor-extensions); [current work](../roadmap/current.md#current-slice) owns follow-up selection.

## Editor label rejection evidence — 2026-10-15

- Revision: uncommitted fix on base `7f4f02e1935b8437d8ece41dbcd3bb88df4986e1`. Source and regression checks: [editor prototype](../../examples/editor-prototype/main.cpp); the host result/source attribution boundary is owned by the [consumer contract](../integrations/path-finder.md#implemented-in-process-panel-fixture). Core APIs and serialized formats are unchanged.
- Configuration: reused Windows x64/MSVC `build-v030-component-effects`, Visual Studio 18 2026, Debug and Release, tests/examples ON and fonts/Vulkan OFF. No external dependency, native window or GPU.
- Demonstrated failure/minimum fix: an open selected-label draft reloaded with a rejecting text measurement service escaped `reload_label` as `FixtureRejection`, terminating the fixture instead of returning diagnostics for retry. The pre-fix focused Debug CTest failed; `editor-label-rejection-before.log` retains the output. Catch only declared presentation rejection around full-tree staging, preserve its diagnostic causes/source, and associate unlocated causes with the candidate edited value span. No mounting, commit or publication occurs on this rejection path.
- Procedure: `cmake --build build-v030-component-effects --config Debug --target tessera_editor_prototype --parallel 1` and the Release equivalent. Run `ctest --test-dir build-v030-component-effects -C Debug -R '^(text_edit|gestures|editor_prototype)$' --output-on-failure --output-log build-v030-component-effects/editor-label-rejection-debug-tests.log` and the Release equivalent with `editor-label-rejection-release-tests.log`.
- Result: all three selected checks passed in each configuration; the rebuilt editor targets emitted no compiler warnings. Documentation/link/ownership checks and `git diff --check` passed. The full 52-check suite and independent source consumers were not rerun for this fixture-only change.
- Boundary checks: targeted measurement and glyph-shaping failures return ordinary result diagnostics with the original cause and candidate property value span. Rejection preserves exact source bytes, preview document, command/inspection generations, selected field, draft text/selection/revision, saved edit, active gesture, empty history, focus, pinned row lifetime/state and ready async observation. Explicit cleanup delivery produces no detachment, and replay/CPU observations of the accepted presentation remain identical. Retrying the same edit accepts one generation and one before/after history entry; existing Unicode editing, cancellation, eligibility, source removal/type restoration, stale targets, async cleanup and deterministic capture assertions run within the editor check.
- Artifacts: Debug/Release editor executables and the logs above under ignored `build-v030-component-effects`; source edits and CPU RGBA8 images stay in memory. Capture uses the fixture's fixed 760×320 viewport, scale one, explicit 10-microsecond tick, placeholder shaping and synthetic scalar rasterizer.
- Limits: declared staging diagnostics for one compatible string edit; no containment of arbitrary/allocation exceptions, runtime/host callback rollback, generalized reload/migration, native editing, real-font/GPU capture, serialized workflow or performance claim. Broader mechanisms stay in [backlog](../roadmap/backlog.md#unscheduled-editor-extensions).

## Editor stale reload evidence — 2026-10-16

- Revision: uncommitted fix on base `7d475f1cc4bf721c215ba8bba9a12c84e5cbd039`. Source and regression checks: [editor prototype](../../examples/editor-prototype/main.cpp); the [consumer contract](../integrations/path-finder.md#implemented-in-process-panel-fixture) owns request identity. Core APIs and serialized formats are unchanged.
- Configuration: reused Windows x64/MSVC `build-v030-component-effects`, Visual Studio 18 2026, Debug and Release, tests/examples ON and fonts/Vulkan OFF.
- Demonstrated failure/minimum fix: retain a selected presentation, publish a newer draft presentation, then request compatible label reload against the retained presentation. The pre-fix focused Debug CTest failed because the stale request was accepted; `editor-stale-reload-before.log` retains the output. Both source reload entries compare command generation and inspection identity against accepted host state before parsing or staging. Foreign editor presentations with equal command counters reject too.
- Procedure: `cmake --build build-v030-component-effects --config Debug --target tessera_editor_prototype --parallel 1` and the Release equivalent; `ctest --test-dir build-v030-component-effects -C Debug -R '^(text_edit|gestures|editor_prototype)$' --output-on-failure --output-log build-v030-component-effects/editor-stale-reload-debug-tests.log` and the Release equivalent with `editor-stale-reload-release-tests.log`.
- Result: all three selected checks passed in each configuration; the rebuilt editor targets emitted no compiler warnings. Full fresh-build release validation is recorded separately below.
- Boundary checks: delayed label/removal requests, stale commands paired with current inspection, foreign inspection with equal command counters and malformed candidates paired with stale requests return `stale_generation` at `/generation`. Rejection preserves exact source, document, command/inspection generations, selected field, draft/selection/revision, saved gesture, empty history, focus, pinned row lifetime/state and loading async observation; explicit delivery queues no detachment. Repeated replay/CPU capture remains identical. Retrying against the accepted presentation publishes one generation, commits one label history entry or cancels the removed owner's draft without history, and preserves or retires its async lifetime accordingly. Existing label/type/restoration regressions run in the same fixture.
- Artifacts: Debug/Release editor executables and the three logs above under ignored `build-v030-component-effects`. Source edits and CPU images remain in memory, using the fixed viewport/scale/time and synthetic text configuration from the editor evidence above.
- Limits: host-entry identity checks for two bounded source operations; no generalized reload/migration, transport, native editor, real-font editor capture or performance claim.

## Editor history shortcut review — 2026-10-16

- Method: source inspection on base `ffd016ee4543cd96d34755599f5d7f9fb7eaa321`; documentation-only scope selection, with no runtime execution or new capability validation. The [editor fixture](../../examples/editor-prototype/main.cpp), [shortcut API](../../include/tessera/commands/shortcuts.hpp) and [tool-panel consumer](../../examples/tool-panel/main.cpp) were reviewed against the [consumer contract](../integrations/path-finder.md#implemented-in-process-panel-fixture).
- Observed friction: `Editor::edit_input` returns unconsumed for Control-modified keys. The editor constructs no `CommandShortcuts` map and has no subsequent chord dispatch. `run_label_history` reaches its keyboard Undo by moving focus to `undo-label` and dispatching logical `Activate`, so that assertion does not exercise Undo from Inspector focus. Source tracing therefore identifies an unconnected consumer path for Control+Z/Control+Shift+Z; it is not an executed failing-test result.
- Existing boundaries: the logical key vocabulary includes Z, exact modifier matching and non-repeating bindings are supplied by `CommandShortcuts`, and the tool panel demonstrates scoped activation-owner routing with a shared sequence stream. Editor `history_command` supplies ordinary accepted-presentation checks, execution, full-tree staging and source-history traversal. The review identifies no required core extension.
- Selection at review: a regression-first host connection of those two chords to label history, retaining Inspector focus, accepted-state rejection and deterministic capture parity. Its scope is retained under [editor follow-ups](../roadmap/backlog.md#unscheduled-editor-extensions), alongside broader native adapters, configurable chords, draft-local history and input frameworks. This source review establishes no implementation or executed-test result for that follow-up.
- Limits: code inspection only. No shortcut implementation, new test result, native keyboard session, serialized workflow or performance evidence is claimed. The implementation evidence for label history remains in the preceding section.

## v0.3.0 release acceptance — 2026-10-16

- Revision: release preparation on base `7d475f1cc4bf721c215ba8bba9a12c84e5cbd039`, including the stale-reload fix above and `VERSION` 0.3.0. Source release contents and bounded scope are in the [changelog](../../CHANGELOG.md#v030--2026-10-16); publication is owned by the [release workflow](../../.github/workflows/release.yml).
- Configuration: fresh `build-release-v030-core` and `build-release-v030-vulkan`, Windows x64, Visual Studio 18 2026, MSVC 19.51.36256.0, MSBuild 18.9.1+a81b43525, Windows SDK 10.0.26100.0, CMake/CTest 4.4.3; tests/examples ON. Core disables both optional modules and uses no dependency toolchain. Combined enables fonts/Vulkan with existing HarfBuzz 10.2.0 and FreeType 2.13.3 through `C:/dev/vcpkg/scripts/buildsystems/vcpkg.cmake`, triplet `x64-windows`, committed Noto fixtures, Vulkan SDK 1.4.350.0 and its Slang 2026.8. No dependencies downloaded.
- Procedure: follow the [core](../guides/development.md#core-build-workflow) and [combined](../guides/development.md#combined-font-and-vulkan-glyph-workflow) workflows in those fresh directories, using explicit SDK/compiler cache paths from the [Vulkan workflow](../guides/development.md#optional-vulkan-workflow). Build each configuration with `cmake --build <directory> --config <configuration> --parallel 4 -- /nodeReuse:false`; run `ctest --test-dir <directory> -C <configuration> --output-on-failure --no-tests=error --output-log <directory>/tests-<configuration-lowercase>.log -j 2`. All four shader stages pass `spirv-val --target-env vulkan1.1`.
- Result: all 52 core checks and all 60 combined checks passed in both Debug and Release, including both independent source imports, optional backend consumers, offscreen GPU rendering, both native menu smokes and font checks. All four builds emitted no compiler warnings. Documentation/link/ownership checks, `git diff --check`, release metadata validation and tag-pinned release-note extraction passed.
- GPU configuration: NVIDIA RTX A5000, vendor `0x10de`, device `0x2231`, driver raw `2504261632`, Vulkan 1.4.329; RGBA8 sRGB offscreen targets and B8G8R8A8 sRGB FIFO native swapchains. GPU fixtures require Khronos validation and synchronization validation; native smokes verify five captures each at scales 1, 1.5 and 1.25.
- Environment: initial sandboxed fresh builds stopped at MSBuild's build-system check and were terminated before compilation; normal execution outside the sandbox completed both configurations. Independent consumer builds and native/GPU checks use the same normal execution boundary.
- Artifacts: per-configuration build and CTest logs, executables and independent consumer phase logs under the two ignored build directories. Combined image captures are under `build-release-v030-vulkan/backends/vulkan/artifacts` and font artifacts under `build-release-v030-vulkan/modules/fonts/artifacts`.
- Acceptance scope: the editor loop uses the ordinary reactive/component/command/input/inspection/full-tree/CPU boundaries, with source diagnostics, gesture cancellation/history, compatible retention, bounded replacement, stale request rejection and async cleanup covered by its scripted fixture. No additional feature implementation is scheduled; [current](../roadmap/current.md) selects a future consumer operation, and broader mechanisms remain in [backlog](../roadmap/backlog.md).
- Limits: one Windows machine/toolchain and the fixed synthetic/real-font fixtures. The editor itself uses placeholder text and synthetic scalar glyphs; combined rendering evidence does not establish a native editor or real-font editor UI. No new physical-device session, other-platform, performance, install/export package or prebuilt binary claim. The source-only 0.x release has no API/ABI compatibility guarantee and is published as a pre-release.
